Zone 36 · 4,358 arc traversals · 7.23 h driving + 0.75 h break = **7.98 h**

> **311 streets on this route are marked [HOLD 40 — still owed].** You would otherwise have driven past them; filming them today is what saves a return trip later in the campaign.
